The additional levy allowing for 5% delinquencies and 2% discount for fiscal year beginning July 1, 2028, shall be on Class I property 0.06 cents; on
Class II property 0.12 cents; and on Class III and Class IV property 0.24 cents; per $100.00 valuation.
The additional levy allowing for 5% delinquencies and 2% discount for fiscal year beginning July 1, 2029, shall be on Class I property 0.06 cents; on
Class II property 0.12 cents; and on Class III and Class IV property 0.24 cents; per $100.00 valuation.
The additional levy allowing for 5% delinquencies and 2% discount for fiscal year beginning July 1, 2030, shall be on Class I property 0.07 cents; on
Class II property 0.14 cents; and on Class III and Class IV property 0.28 cents; per $100.00 valuation.
That in the event the separate and aggregate assessed valuation of each class of taxable property within the County of Harrison increases during the
term of the special levy, the levy rate shall be adjusted so that the projected tax collection will not exceed the maximum sum of: $100,000 for the
fiscal year beginning July 1, 2027; $103,000 for the fiscal year beginning July 1, 2028; $106,090 for the fiscal year beginning July 1, 2029; and
$109,272 for the fiscal year beginning July 1, 2030.
Examples of authorized uses of levy funds are as follows: General operating expenses, building, infrastructure, and equipment maintenance and
repairs, promotion of tourism and historical preservation, acquisition and upgrades of equipment, property or structures, improvements and
modifications to land and facilities, and matching funds to obtain federal funds or other available funds. The Harrison County Commission will each
year provide the amount of funds from the levy to the Historical Preservation Alliance in the amounts as set forth in this order. Further, in relation to
the expenditure of funds, the Historical Preservation Alliance shall quarterly account for all expenditures, costs, and requisitions to the Harrison
County Commission; and no levy funds shall be distributed to the Historical Preservation Alliance without written proof being provided to, and
reviewed by, the Harrison County Commission. The Historical Preservation Alliance shall reimburse the Harrison County Commission for any and all
levy funds determined to have been expended for an unauthorized purpose.
In the event the Historical Preservation Alliance would cease to exist, for whatever reason, or, if the amount of funds collected exceed the
approximate amount, the Harrison County Commission will follow all necessary legal and governmental requirements to ensure that any funding
previously allocated to said entity may be repurposed; and that such finding may be repurposed for use in county special and/or improvements
projects; or otherwise that such funds be eliminated from collection consistent with West Virginia law.
For, the additional levy to provide funding for The Historical Preservation Alliance
Against, the additional levy to provide funding for The Historical Preservation Alliance
Special Levy Election
Community Health Organizations within Harrison County
Lowe Public Library, Southern Area Public Library, Nutter Fort Public Library
Harrison County Commission for Harrison County Special Projects
According to the Order of the Harrison County Commission, adopted on the 31st day of August, 2026, A Special election will be held to authorize
additional levies, allowing for 5% delinquencies and 2% discount, for the fiscal years beginning July 1, 2027, July 1, 2028, July 1, 2029 and July 1,
2030 for the purpose of providing for the continued operation of the following organizations in the following amounts:
Agency Fiscal Year 27/28 Fiscal Year 28/29 Fiscal Year 29/30 Fiscal Year 30/31
Community Health $880,000 $906,400 $933,592 $961,600
Lowe Public Library $10,000 $10,300 $10,609 $10,927
Southern Area Public Library $10,000 $10,300 $10,609 $10,927
Nutter Fort Public Library $10,000 $10,300 $10,609 $10,927
Harrison County Special Projects $450,000 $463,500 $477,405 $491,727
Totals $1,360,000 $1,400,800 $1,442,824 $1,486,108
The additional levy allowing for 5% delinquencies and 2% discount for fiscal year beginning July 1, 2027, shall be on Class I property 0.88 cents; on
Class II property 1.76 cents; and on Class III and Class IV property 3.52 cents; per $100.00 valuation.
The additional levy allowing for 5% delinquencies and 2% discount for fiscal year beginning July 1, 2028, shall be on Class I property 0.91 cents; on
Class II property 1.82 cents; and on Class III and Class IV property 3.64 cents; per $100.00 valuation.
The additional levy allowing for 5% delinquencies and 2% discount for fiscal year beginning July 1, 2029, shall be on Class I property 0.94 cents; on
Class II property 1.88 cents; and on Class III and Class IV property 3.76 cents; per $100.00 valuation.
The additional levy allowing for 5% delinquencies and 2% discount for fiscal year beginning July 1, 2030, shall be on Class I property 0.96 cents; on
Class II property 1.92 cents; and on Class III and Class IV property 3.84 cents; per $100.00 valuation.
That in the event the separate and aggregate assessed valuation of each class of taxable property within the County of Harrison increases during the
term of the special levy, the levy rate shall be adjusted so that the projected tax collection will not exceed the maximum sum of: $1,360,000 for the
fiscal year beginning July 1, 2027; $1,400,800 for the fiscal year beginning July 1, 2028; $1,442,824 for the fiscal year beginning July 1, 2029; and
$1,486,108 for the fiscal year beginning July 1, 2030.
Examples of acceptable usage of funds are as follows: General operating expenses, building, infrastructure, and equipment maintenance and
repairs, acquisition and upgrades of equipment, property or structures, improvements and modifications to land and facilities, and matching funds to
obtain federal funds or other available funds. The Harrison County Commission will each year provide the amount of funds from the levy to the
Clarksburg/Harrison Health Department, Lowe Public Library, Southern Area Public Library, Nutter Fort Public Library, and Harrison County for
Harrison County Special Projects. Funds for the remaining organizations will be distributed by the Clarksburg/Harrison Health Department to each
organization in the amounts as set forth in this order.
In the event any of these entities would cease to exist, for whatever reason, or, if the amount of funds collected exceed the approximate amount, the
Harrison County Commission will follow all necessary legal and governmental requirements to ensure that any funding previously allocated to said
entity may be redistributed to one or more of the remaining entities, on an equitable basis. If the amount of funds collected is less than the
anticipated approximate amount, each beneficiarys distribution amount will be reduced equitably.
In the event that sufficient State, Federal, or other special funds become available to provide monies for any of the above purposes, or the Harrison
County Commissions anticipated expenses for each stated purpose are lower than projected, the levy monies specified for these purposes may be
used for the general operation and improvement of the county. The Harrison County Commission is hereby authorized and empowered to expend,
during the term of this Levy, the funds, if any, occurring in excess of the amount needed for any of the above stated purposes for the enrichment,
supplementation, improvement and advancement of all county programs, operations and facilities in Harrison County, and other purposes pertinent
to the operation of the county.
Yes, for continued funding for Harrison County Commission for Community Health Organizations within Harrison County, Lowe
Public Library, Southern Area Public Library, Nutter Fort Public Library, and the Harrison County Commission for Harrison County
Special Projects
No, for continued funding for Harrison County Commission for Community Health Organizations within Harrison County, Lowe
Public Library, Southern Area Public Library, Nutter Fort Public Library, and the Harrison County Commission for Harrison County
Special Projects

Amendment No. 1: Citizenship Requirement
to Vote in West Virginia Elections
Amendment
Summary of Purpose: This amendment
provides that in all elections held in West Virginia
only citizens of the United States who are
citizens of this state are qualified to vote.

Class II property 0.12 cents; and on Class III and Class IV property 0.24 cents; per $100.00 valuation.

during the term of this Levy, the funds, if any, occurring in excess of the amount needed for any of the above stated purposes for the enrichment,
supplementation, improvement and advancement of all county programs, operations and facilities in Harrison County, and other purposes pertinent
to the operation of the county.
Yes, for continued funding for Harrison County Commission for Community Health Organizations within Harrison County, Lowe
Public Library, Southern Area Public Library, Nutter Fort Public Library, and the Harrison County Commission for Harrison County
Special Projects
No, for continued funding for Harrison County Commission for Community Health Organizations within Harrison County, Lowe
Public Library, Southern Area Public Library, Nutter Fort Public Library, and the Harrison County Commission for Harrison County
Special Projects

only citizens of the United States who are

OFFICIAL LEVY BALLOT
Special Levy Election
Historical Preservation Alliance
According to the Order of the Harrison County Commission, adopted on the 31st day of August, 2026, A Special election will be held to authorize
additional levies, allowing for 5% delinquencies and 2% discount, for the fiscal years beginning July 1, 2027, July 1, 2028, July 1, 2029 and July 1,
2030 for the purpose of providing funding for the following organization in the following amounts:
Agency Fiscal Year 27/28 Fiscal Year 28/29 Fiscal Year 29/30 Fiscal Year 30/31
Historical Preservation Alliance $100,000 $103,000 $106,090 $109,272
Totals $100,000 $103,000 $106,090 $109,272
The additional levy allowing for 5% delinquencies and 2% discount for fiscal year beginning July 1, 2027, shall be on Class I property 0.06 cents; on
Class II property 0.12 cents; and on Class III and Class IV property 0.24 cents; per $100.00 valuation.
The additional levy allowing for 5% delinquencies and 2% discount for fiscal year beginning July 1, 2028, shall be on Class I property 0.06 cents; on
Class II property 0.12 cents; and on Class III and Class IV property 0.24 cents; per $100.00 valuation.
The additional levy allowing for 5% delinquencies and 2% discount for fiscal year beginning July 1, 2029, shall be on Class I property 0.06 cents; on
Class II property 0.12 cents; and on Class III and Class IV property 0.24 cents; per $100.00 valuation.
The additional levy allowing for 5% delinquencies and 2% discount for fiscal year beginning July 1, 2030, shall be on Class I property 0.07 cents; on
Class II property 0.14 cents; and on Class III and Class IV property 0.28 cents; per $100.00 valuation.
That in the event the separate and aggregate assessed valuation of each class of taxable property within the County of Harrison increases during the
term of the special levy, the levy rate shall be adjusted so that the projected tax collection will not exceed the maximum sum of: $100,000 for the
fiscal year beginning July 1, 2027; $103,000 for the fiscal year beginning July 1, 2028; $106,090 for the fiscal year beginning July 1, 2029; and
$109,272 for the fiscal year beginning July 1, 2030.
Examples of authorized uses of levy funds are as follows: General operating expenses, building, infrastructure, and equipment maintenance and
repairs, promotion of tourism and historical preservation, acquisition and upgrades of equipment, property or structures, improvements and
modifications to land and facilities, and matching funds to obtain federal funds or other available funds. The Harrison County Commission will each
year provide the amount of funds from the levy to the Historical Preservation Alliance in the amounts as set forth in this order. Further, in relation to
the expenditure of funds, the Historical Preservation Alliance shall quarterly account for all expenditures, costs, and requisitions to the Harrison
County Commission; and no levy funds shall be distributed to the Historical Preservation Alliance without written proof being provided to, and
reviewed by, the Harrison County Commission. The Historical Preservation Alliance shall reimburse the Harrison County Commission for any and all
levy funds determined to have been expended for an unauthorized purpose.
In the event the Historical Preservation Alliance would cease to exist, for whatever reason, or, if the amount of funds collected exceed the
approximate amount, the Harrison County Commission will follow all necessary legal and governmental requirements to ensure that any funding
previously allocated to said entity may be repurposed; and that such finding may be repurposed for use in county special and/or improvements
projects; or otherwise that such funds be eliminated from collection consistent with West Virginia law.
For, the additional levy to provide funding for The Historical Preservation Alliance
Against, the additional levy to provide funding for The Historical Preservation Alliance
Special Levy Election
Community Health Organizations within Harrison County
Lowe Public Library, Southern Area Public Library, Nutter Fort Public Library
Harrison County Commission for Harrison County Special Projects
According to the Order of the Harrison County Commission, adopted on the 31st day of August, 2026, A Special election will be held to authorize
additional levies, allowing for 5% delinquencies and 2% discount, for the fiscal years beginning July 1, 2027, July 1, 2028, July 1, 2029 and July 1,
2030 for the purpose of providing for the continued operation of the following organizations in the following amounts:
Agency Fiscal Year 27/28 Fiscal Year 28/29 Fiscal Year 29/30 Fiscal Year 30/31
Community Health $880,000 $906,400 $933,592 $961,600
Lowe Public Library $10,000 $10,300 $10,609 $10,927
Southern Area Public Library $10,000 $10,300 $10,609 $10,927
Nutter Fort Public Library $10,000 $10,300 $10,609 $10,927
Harrison County Special Projects $450,000 $463,500 $477,405 $491,727
Totals $1,360,000 $1,400,800 $1,442,824 $1,486,108
The additional levy allowing for 5% delinquencies and 2% discount for fiscal year beginning July 1, 2027, shall be on Class I property 0.88 cents; on
Class II property 1.76 cents; and on Class III and Class IV property 3.52 cents; per $100.00 valuation.
The additional levy allowing for 5% delinquencies and 2% discount for fiscal year beginning July 1, 2028, shall be on Class I property 0.91 cents; on
Class II property 1.82 cents; and on Class III and Class IV property 3.64 cents; per $100.00 valuation.
The additional levy allowing for 5% delinquencies and 2% discount for fiscal year beginning July 1, 2029, shall be on Class I property 0.94 cents; on
Class II property 1.88 cents; and on Class III and Class IV property 3.76 cents; per $100.00 valuation.
The additional levy allowing for 5% delinquencies and 2% discount for fiscal year beginning July 1, 2030, shall be on Class I property 0.96 cents; on
Class II property 1.92 cents; and on Class III and Class IV property 3.84 cents; per $100.00 valuation.
That in the event the separate and aggregate assessed valuation of each class of taxable property within the County of Harrison increases during the
term of the special levy, the levy rate shall be adjusted so that the projected tax collection will not exceed the maximum sum of: $1,360,000 for the
fiscal year beginning July 1, 2027; $1,400,800 for the fiscal year beginning July 1, 2028; $1,442,824 for the fiscal year beginning July 1, 2029; and
$1,486,108 for the fiscal year beginning July 1, 2030.
Examples of acceptable usage of funds are as follows: General operating expenses, building, infrastructure, and equipment maintenance and
repairs, acquisition and upgrades of equipment, property or structures, improvements and modifications to land and facilities, and matching funds to
obtain federal funds or other available funds. The Harrison County Commission will each year provide the amount of funds from the levy to the
Clarksburg/Harrison Health Department, Lowe Public Library, Southern Area Public Library, Nutter Fort Public Library, and Harrison County for
Harrison County Special Projects. Funds for the remaining organizations will be distributed by the Clarksburg/Harrison Health Department to each
organization in the amounts as set forth in this order.
In the event any of these entities would cease to exist, for whatever reason, or, if the amount of funds collected exceed the approximate amount, the
Harrison County Commission will follow all necessary legal and governmental requirements to ensure that any funding previously allocated to said
entity may be redistributed to one or more of the remaining entities, on an equitable basis. If the amount of funds collected is less than the
anticipated approximate amount, each beneficiarys distribution amount will be reduced equitably.
In the event that sufficient State, Federal, or other special funds become available to provide monies for any of the above purposes, or the Harrison
County Commissions anticipated expenses for each stated purpose are lower than projected, the levy monies specified for these purposes may be
used for the general operation and improvement of the county. The Harrison County Commission is hereby authorized and empowered to expend,
during the term of this Levy, the funds, if any, occurring in excess of the amount needed for any of the above stated purposes for the enrichment,
supplementation, improvement and advancement of all county programs, operations and facilities in Harrison County, and other purposes pertinent
to the operation of the county.
Yes, for continued funding for Harrison County Commission for Community Health Organizations within Harrison County, Lowe
Public Library, Southern Area Public Library, Nutter Fort Public Library, and the Harrison County Commission for Harrison County
Special Projects
No, for continued funding for Harrison County Commission for Community Health Organizations within Harrison County, Lowe
Public Library, Southern Area Public Library, Nutter Fort Public Library, and the Harrison County Commission for Harrison County
Special Projects

fiscal year beginning July 1, 2027; $103,000 for the fiscal year beginning July 1, 2028; $106,090 for the fiscal year beginning July 1, 2029; and
$109,272 for the fiscal year beginning July 1, 2030.
Examples of authorized uses of levy funds are as follows: General operating expenses, building, infrastructure, and equipment maintenance and
repairs, promotion of tourism and historical preservation, acquisition and upgrades of equipment, property or structures, improvements and
modifications to land and facilities, and matching funds to obtain federal funds or other available funds. The Harrison County Commission will each
year provide the amount of funds from the levy to the Historical Preservation Alliance in the amounts as set forth in this order. Further, in relation to
the expenditure of funds, the Historical Preservation Alliance shall quarterly account for all expenditures, costs, and requisitions to the Harrison
County Commission; and no levy funds shall be distributed to the Historical Preservation Alliance without written proof being provided to, and
reviewed by, the Harrison County Commission. The Historical Preservation Alliance shall reimburse the Harrison County Commission for any and all
levy funds determined to have been expended for an unauthorized purpose.
In the event the Historical Preservation Alliance would cease to exist, for whatever reason, or, if the amount of funds collected exceed the
approximate amount, the Harrison County Commission will follow all necessary legal and governmental requirements to ensure that any funding
previously allocated to said entity may be repurposed; and that such finding may be repurposed for use in county special and/or improvements
projects; or otherwise that such funds be eliminated from collection consistent with West Virginia law.
For, the additional levy to provide funding for The Historical Preservation Alliance
Against, the additional levy to provide funding for The Historical Preservation Alliance
Special Levy Election
Community Health Organizations within Harrison County
Lowe Public Library, Southern Area Public Library, Nutter Fort Public Library
Harrison County Commission for Harrison County Special Projects
According to the Order of the Harrison County Commission, adopted on the 31st day of August, 2026, A Special election will be held to authorize
additional levies, allowing for 5% delinquencies and 2% discount, for the fiscal years beginning July 1, 2027, July 1, 2028, July 1, 2029 and July 1,
2030 for the purpose of providing for the continued operation of the following organizations in the following amounts:
Agency Fiscal Year 27/28 Fiscal Year 28/29 Fiscal Year 29/30 Fiscal Year 30/31
Community Health $880,000 $906,400 $933,592 $961,600
Lowe Public Library $10,000 $10,300 $10,609 $10,927
Southern Area Public Library $10,000 $10,300 $10,609 $10,927
Nutter Fort Public Library $10,000 $10,300 $10,609 $10,927
Harrison County Special Projects $450,000 $463,500 $477,405 $491,727
Totals $1,360,000 $1,400,800 $1,442,824 $1,486,108
The additional levy allowing for 5% delinquencies and 2% discount for fiscal year beginning July 1, 2027, shall be on Class I property 0.88 cents; on
Class II property 1.76 cents; and on Class III and Class IV property 3.52 cents; per $100.00 valuation.
The additional levy allowing for 5% delinquencies and 2% discount for fiscal year beginning July 1, 2028, shall be on Class I property 0.91 cents; on
Class II property 1.82 cents; and on Class III and Class IV property 3.64 cents; per $100.00 valuation.
The additional levy allowing for 5% delinquencies and 2% discount for fiscal year beginning July 1, 2029, shall be on Class I property 0.94 cents; on
Class II property 1.88 cents; and on Class III and Class IV property 3.76 cents; per $100.00 valuation.
The additional levy allowing for 5% delinquencies and 2% discount for fiscal year beginning July 1, 2030, shall be on Class I property 0.96 cents; on
Class II property 1.92 cents; and on Class III and Class IV property 3.84 cents; per $100.00 valuation.
That in the event the separate and aggregate assessed valuation of each class of taxable property within the County of Harrison increases during the
term of the special levy, the levy rate shall be adjusted so that the projected tax collection will not exceed the maximum sum of: $1,360,000 for the
fiscal year beginning July 1, 2027; $1,400,800 for the fiscal year beginning July 1, 2028; $1,442,824 for the fiscal year beginning July 1, 2029; and
$1,486,108 for the fiscal year beginning July 1, 2030.
Examples of acceptable usage of funds are as follows: General operating expenses, building, infrastructure, and equipment maintenance and
repairs, acquisition and upgrades of equipment, property or structures, improvements and modifications to land and facilities, and matching funds to
obtain federal funds or other available funds. The Harrison County Commission will each year provide the amount of funds from the levy to the
Clarksburg/Harrison Health Department, Lowe Public Library, Southern Area Public Library, Nutter Fort Public Library, and Harrison County for
Harrison County Special Projects. Funds for the remaining organizations will be distributed by the Clarksburg/Harrison Health Department to each
organization in the amounts as set forth in this order.
In the event any of these entities would cease to exist, for whatever reason, or, if the amount of funds collected exceed the approximate amount, the
Harrison County Commission will follow all necessary legal and governmental requirements to ensure that any funding previously allocated to said
entity may be redistributed to one or more of the remaining entities, on an equitable basis. If the amount of funds collected is less than the
anticipated approximate amount, each beneficiarys distribution amount will be reduced equitably.
In the event that sufficient State, Federal, or other special funds become available to provide monies for any of the above purposes, or the Harrison
County Commissions anticipated expenses for each stated purpose are lower than projected, the levy monies specified for these purposes may be
used for the general operation and improvement of the county. The Harrison County Commission is hereby authorized and empowered to expend,
during the term of this Levy, the funds, if any, occurring in excess of the amount needed for any of the above stated purposes for the enrichment,
supplementation, improvement and advancement of all county programs, operations and facilities in Harrison County, and other purposes pertinent
to the operation of the county.
Yes, for continued funding for Harrison County Commission for Community Health Organizations within Harrison County, Lowe
Public Library, Southern Area Public Library, Nutter Fort Public Library, and the Harrison County Commission for Harrison County
Special Projects
No, for continued funding for Harrison County Commission for Community Health Organizations within Harrison County, Lowe
Public Library, Southern Area Public Library, Nutter Fort Public Library, and the Harrison County Commission for Harrison County
Special Projects

Amendment No. 1: Citizenship Requirement
to Vote in West Virginia Elections
Amendment
Summary of Purpose: This amendment
provides that in all elections held in West Virginia
only citizens of the United States who are
citizens of this state are qualified to vote.

Special Levy Election
Historical Preservation Alliance
According to the Order of the Harrison County Commission, adopted on the 31st day of August, 2026, A Special election will be held to authorize
additional levies, allowing for 5% delinquencies and 2% discount, for the fiscal years beginning July 1, 2027, July 1, 2028, July 1, 2029 and July 1,
2030 for the purpose of providing funding for the following organization in the following amounts:
Agency Fiscal Year 27/28 Fiscal Year 28/29 Fiscal Year 29/30 Fiscal Year 30/31
Historical Preservation Alliance $100,000 $103,000 $106,090 $109,272
Totals $100,000 $103,000 $106,090 $109,272
The additional levy allowing for 5% delinquencies and 2% discount for fiscal year beginning July 1, 2027, shall be on Class I property 0.06 cents; on
Class II property 0.12 cents; and on Class III and Class IV property 0.24 cents; per $100.00 valuation.
The additional levy allowing for 5% delinquencies and 2% discount for fiscal year beginning July 1, 2028, shall be on Class I property 0.06 cents; on
Class II property 0.12 cents; and on Class III and Class IV property 0.24 cents; per $100.00 valuation.
The additional levy allowing for 5% delinquencies and 2% discount for fiscal year beginning July 1, 2029, shall be on Class I property 0.06 cents; on
Class II property 0.12 cents; and on Class III and Class IV property 0.24 cents; per $100.00 valuation.
The additional levy allowing for 5% delinquencies and 2% discount for fiscal year beginning July 1, 2030, shall be on Class I property 0.07 cents; on
Class II property 0.14 cents; and on Class III and Class IV property 0.28 cents; per $100.00 valuation.
That in the event the separate and aggregate assessed valuation of each class of taxable property within the County of Harrison increases during the
term of the special levy, the levy rate shall be adjusted so that the projected tax collection will not exceed the maximum sum of: $100,000 for the
fiscal year beginning July 1, 2027; $103,000 for the fiscal year beginning July 1, 2028; $106,090 for the fiscal year beginning July 1, 2029; and
$109,272 for the fiscal year beginning July 1, 2030.
Examples of authorized uses of levy funds are as follows: General operating expenses, building, infrastructure, and equipment maintenance and
repairs, promotion of tourism and historical preservation, acquisition and upgrades of equipment, property or structures, improvements and
modifications to land and facilities, and matching funds to obtain federal funds or other available funds. The Harrison County Commission will each
year provide the amount of funds from the levy to the Historical Preservation Alliance in the amounts as set forth in this order. Further, in relation to
the expenditure of funds, the Historical Preservation Alliance shall quarterly account for all expenditures, costs, and requisitions to the Harrison
County Commission; and no levy funds shall be distributed to the Historical Preservation Alliance without written proof being provided to, and
reviewed by, the Harrison County Commission. The Historical Preservation Alliance shall reimburse the Harrison County Commission for any and all

approximate amount, the Harrison County Commission will follow all necessary legal and governmental requirements to ensure that any funding
previously allocated to said entity may be repurposed; and that such finding may be repurposed for use in county special and/or improvements

BALLOT ON CONSTITUTIONAL
AMENDMENTS
Amendment No. 1: Citizenship Requirement
to Vote in West Virginia Elections
Amendment
Summary of Purpose: This amendment
provides that in all elections held in West Virginia
only citizens of the United States who are
citizens of this state are qualified to vote.
Instructions to Voters:
To vote in favor of the Amendment submitted,
darken the oval next to "FOR". To vote against
the Amendment, darken the oval next to
"AGAINST".
FOR
AGAINST

OFFICIAL LEVY BALLOT
Special Levy Election
Historical Preservation Alliance
According to the Order of the Harrison County Commission, adopted on the 31st day of August, 2026, A Special election will be held to authorize
additional levies, allowing for 5% delinquencies and 2% discount, for the fiscal years beginning July 1, 2027, July 1, 2028, July 1, 2029 and July 1,
2030 for the purpose of providing funding for the following organization in the following amounts:
Agency Fiscal Year 27/28 Fiscal Year 28/29 Fiscal Year 29/30 Fiscal Year 30/31
Historical Preservation Alliance $100,000 $103,000 $106,090 $109,272
Totals $100,000 $103,000 $106,090 $109,272
The additional levy allowing for 5% delinquencies and 2% discount for fiscal year beginning July 1, 2027, shall be on Class I property 0.06 cents; on
Class II property 0.12 cents; and on Class III and Class IV property 0.24 cents; per $100.00 valuation.
The additional levy allowing for 5% delinquencies and 2% discount for fiscal year beginning July 1, 2028, shall be on Class I property 0.06 cents; on
Class II property 0.12 cents; and on Class III and Class IV property 0.24 cents; per $100.00 valuation.
The additional levy allowing for 5% delinquencies and 2% discount for fiscal year beginning July 1, 2029, shall be on Class I property 0.06 cents; on
Class II property 0.12 cents; and on Class III and Class IV property 0.24 cents; per $100.00 valuation.
The additional levy allowing for 5% delinquencies and 2% discount for fiscal year beginning July 1, 2030, shall be on Class I property 0.07 cents; on
Class II property 0.14 cents; and on Class III and Class IV property 0.28 cents; per $100.00 valuation.
That in the event the separate and aggregate assessed valuation of each class of taxable property within the County of Harrison increases during the
term of the special levy, the levy rate shall be adjusted so that the projected tax collection will not exceed the maximum sum of: $100,000 for the
fiscal year beginning July 1, 2027; $103,000 for the fiscal year beginning July 1, 2028; $106,090 for the fiscal year beginning July 1, 2029; and
$109,272 for the fiscal year beginning July 1, 2030.
Examples of authorized uses of levy funds are as follows: General operating expenses, building, infrastructure, and equipment maintenance and
repairs, promotion of tourism and historical preservation, acquisition and upgrades of equipment, property or structures, improvements and
modifications to land and facilities, and matching funds to obtain federal funds or other available funds. The Harrison County Commission will each
year provide the amount of funds from the levy to the Historical Preservation Alliance in the amounts as set forth in this order. Further, in relation to
the expenditure of funds, the Historical Preservation Alliance shall quarterly account for all expenditures, costs, and requisitions to the Harrison
County Commission; and no levy funds shall be distributed to the Historical Preservation Alliance without written proof being provided to, and
reviewed by, the Harrison County Commission. The Historical Preservation Alliance shall reimburse the Harrison County Commission for any and all
levy funds determined to have been expended for an unauthorized purpose.
In the event the Historical Preservation Alliance would cease to exist, for whatever reason, or, if the amount of funds collected exceed the
approximate amount, the Harrison County Commission will follow all necessary legal and governmental requirements to ensure that any funding
previously allocated to said entity may be repurposed; and that such finding may be repurposed for use in county special and/or improvements
projects; or otherwise that such funds be eliminated from collection consistent with West Virginia law.
For, the additional levy to provide funding for The Historical Preservation Alliance
Against, the additional levy to provide funding for The Historical Preservation Alliance
Special Levy Election
Community Health Organizations within Harrison County
Lowe Public Library, Southern Area Public Library, Nutter Fort Public Library
Harrison County Commission for Harrison County Special Projects
According to the Order of the Harrison County Commission, adopted on the 31st day of August, 2026, A Special election will be held to authorize
additional levies, allowing for 5% delinquencies and 2% discount, for the fiscal years beginning July 1, 2027, July 1, 2028, July 1, 2029 and July 1,
2030 for the purpose of providing for the continued operation of the following organizations in the following amounts:
Agency Fiscal Year 27/28 Fiscal Year 28/29 Fiscal Year 29/30 Fiscal Year 30/31
Community Health $880,000 $906,400 $933,592 $961,600
Lowe Public Library $10,000 $10,300 $10,609 $10,927
Southern Area Public Library $10,000 $10,300 $10,609 $10,927
Nutter Fort Public Library $10,000 $10,300 $10,609 $10,927
Harrison County Special Projects $450,000 $463,500 $477,405 $491,727
Totals $1,360,000 $1,400,800 $1,442,824 $1,486,108
The additional levy allowing for 5% delinquencies and 2% discount for fiscal year beginning July 1, 2027, shall be on Class I property 0.88 cents; on
Class II property 1.76 cents; and on Class III and Class IV property 3.52 cents; per $100.00 valuation.
The additional levy allowing for 5% delinquencies and 2% discount for fiscal year beginning July 1, 2028, shall be on Class I property 0.91 cents; on
Class II property 1.82 cents; and on Class III and Class IV property 3.64 cents; per $100.00 valuation.
The additional levy allowing for 5% delinquencies and 2% discount for fiscal year beginning July 1, 2029, shall be on Class I property 0.94 cents; on
Class II property 1.88 cents; and on Class III and Class IV property 3.76 cents; per $100.00 valuation.
The additional levy allowing for 5% delinquencies and 2% discount for fiscal year beginning July 1, 2030, shall be on Class I property 0.96 cents; on
Class II property 1.92 cents; and on Class III and Class IV property 3.84 cents; per $100.00 valuation.
That in the event the separate and aggregate assessed valuation of each class of taxable property within the County of Harrison increases during the
term of the special levy, the levy rate shall be adjusted so that the projected tax collection will not exceed the maximum sum of: $1,360,000 for the
fiscal year beginning July 1, 2027; $1,400,800 for the fiscal year beginning July 1, 2028; $1,442,824 for the fiscal year beginning July 1, 2029; and
$1,486,108 for the fiscal year beginning July 1, 2030.
Examples of acceptable usage of funds are as follows: General operating expenses, building, infrastructure, and equipment maintenance and
repairs, acquisition and upgrades of equipment, property or structures, improvements and modifications to land and facilities, and matching funds to
obtain federal funds or other available funds. The Harrison County Commission will each year provide the amount of funds from the levy to the
Clarksburg/Harrison Health Department, Lowe Public Library, Southern Area Public Library, Nutter Fort Public Library, and Harrison County for
Harrison County Special Projects. Funds for the remaining organizations will be distributed by the Clarksburg/Harrison Health Department to each
organization in the amounts as set forth in this order.
In the event any of these entities would cease to exist, for whatever reason, or, if the amount of funds collected exceed the approximate amount, the
Harrison County Commission will follow all necessary legal and governmental requirements to ensure that any funding previously allocated to said
entity may be redistributed to one or more of the remaining entities, on an equitable basis. If the amount of funds collected is less than the
anticipated approximate amount, each beneficiarys distribution amount will be reduced equitably.
In the event that sufficient State, Federal, or other special funds become available to provide monies for any of the above purposes, or the Harrison
County Commissions anticipated expenses for each stated purpose are lower than projected, the levy monies specified for these purposes may be
used for the general operation and improvement of the county. The Harrison County Commission is hereby authorized and empowered to expend,
during the term of this Levy, the funds, if any, occurring in excess of the amount needed for any of the above stated purposes for the enrichment,
supplementation, improvement and advancement of all county programs, operations and facilities in Harrison County, and other purposes pertinent
to the operation of the county.
Yes, for continued funding for Harrison County Commission for Community Health Organizations within Harrison County, Lowe
Public Library, Southern Area Public Library, Nutter Fort Public Library, and the Harrison County Commission for Harrison County
Special Projects
No, for continued funding for Harrison County Commission for Community Health Organizations within Harrison County, Lowe
Public Library, Southern Area Public Library, Nutter Fort Public Library, and the Harrison County Commission for Harrison County
Special Projects
S

projects; or otherwise that such funds be eliminated from collection consistent with West Virginia law.
For, the additional levy to provide funding for The Historical Preservation Alliance
Against, the additional levy to provide funding for The Historical Preservation Alliance
Special Levy Election
Community Health Organizations within Harrison County
Lowe Public Library, Southern Area Public Library, Nutter Fort Public Library
Harrison County Commission for Harrison County Special Projects
According to the Order of the Harrison County Commission, adopted on the 31st day of August, 2026, A Special election will be held to authorize
additional levies, allowing for 5% delinquencies and 2% discount, for the fiscal years beginning July 1, 2027, July 1, 2028, July 1, 2029 and July 1,
2030 for the purpose of providing for the continued operation of the following organizations in the following amounts:
Agency Fiscal Year 27/28 Fiscal Year 28/29 Fiscal Year 29/30 Fiscal Year 30/31
Community Health $880,000 $906,400 $933,592 $961,600
Lowe Public Library $10,000 $10,300 $10,609 $10,927
Southern Area Public Library $10,000 $10,300 $10,609 $10,927
Nutter Fort Public Library $10,000 $10,300 $10,609 $10,927
Harrison County Special Projects $450,000 $463,500 $477,405 $491,727
Totals $1,360,000 $1,400,800 $1,442,824 $1,486,108
The additional levy allowing for 5% delinquencies and 2% discount for fiscal year beginning July 1, 2027, shall be on Class I property 0.88 cents; on
Class II property 1.76 cents; and on Class III and Class IV property 3.52 cents; per $100.00 valuation.
The additional levy allowing for 5% delinquencies and 2% discount for fiscal year beginning July 1, 2028, shall be on Class I property 0.91 cents; on
Class II property 1.82 cents; and on Class III and Class IV property 3.64 cents; per $100.00 valuation.
The additional levy allowing for 5% delinquencies and 2% discount for fiscal year beginning July 1, 2029, shall be on Class I property 0.94 cents; on
Class II property 1.88 cents; and on Class III and Class IV property 3.76 cents; per $100.00 valuation.
The additional levy allowing for 5% delinquencies and 2% discount for fiscal year beginning July 1, 2030, shall be on Class I property 0.96 cents; on
Class II property 1.92 cents; and on Class III and Class IV property 3.84 cents; per $100.00 valuation.
That in the event the separate and aggregate assessed valuation of each class of taxable property within the County of Harrison increases during the
term of the special levy, the levy rate shall be adjusted so that the projected tax collection will not exceed the maximum sum of: $1,360,000 for the
fiscal year beginning July 1, 2027; $1,400,800 for the fiscal year beginning July 1, 2028; $1,442,824 for the fiscal year beginning July 1, 2029; and
$1,486,108 for the fiscal year beginning July 1, 2030.
Examples of acceptable usage of funds are as follows: General operating expenses, building, infrastructure, and equipment maintenance and
repairs, acquisition and upgrades of equipment, property or structures, improvements and modifications to land and facilities, and matching funds to
obtain federal funds or other available funds. The Harrison County Commission will each year provide the amount of funds from the levy to the
Clarksburg/Harrison Health Department, Lowe Public Library, Southern Area Public Library, Nutter Fort Public Library, and Harrison County for
Harrison County Special Projects. Funds for the remaining organizations will be distributed by the Clarksburg/Harrison Health Department to each
organization in the amounts as set forth in this order.
In the event any of these entities would cease to exist, for whatever reason, or, if the amount of funds collected exceed the approximate amount, the
Harrison County Commission will follow all necessary legal and governmental requirements to ensure that any funding previously allocated to said
entity may be redistributed to one or more of the remaining entities, on an equitable basis. If the amount of funds collected is less than the
anticipated approximate amount, each beneficiarys distribution amount will be reduced equitably.
In the event that sufficient State, Federal, or other special funds become available to provide monies for any of the above purposes, or the Harrison
County Commissions anticipated expenses for each stated purpose are lower than projected, the levy monies specified for these purposes may be
used for the general operation and improvement of the county. The Harrison County Commission is hereby authorized and empowered to expend,
during the term of this Levy, the funds, if any, occurring in excess of the amount needed for any of the above stated purposes for the enrichment,
supplementation, improvement and advancement of all county programs, operations and facilities in Harrison County, and other purposes pertinent
to the operation of the county.
Yes, for continued funding for Harrison County Commission for Community Health Organizations within Harrison County, Lowe
Public Library, Southern Area Public Library, Nutter Fort Public Library, and the Harrison County Commission for Harrison County
Special Projects
No, for continued funding for Harrison County Commission for Community Health Organizations within Harrison County, Lowe
Public Library, Southern Area Public Library, Nutter Fort Public Library, and the Harrison County Commission for Harrison County
Special Projects

AMENDMENTS
Amendment No. 1: Citizenship Requirement
to Vote in West Virginia Elections
Amendment
Summary of Purpose: This amendment
provides that in all elections held in West Virginia
only citizens of the United States who are
citizens of this state are qualified to vote.
Instructions to Voters:
To vote in favor of the Amendment submitted,
darken the oval next to "FOR". To vote against
the Amendment, darken the oval next to
"AGAINST".

OFFICIAL LEVY BALLOT
Special Levy Election
Historical Preservation Alliance
According to the Order of the Harrison County Commission, adopted on the 31st day of August, 2026, A Special election will be held to authorize
additional levies, allowing for 5% delinquencies and 2% discount, for the fiscal years beginning July 1, 2027, July 1, 2028, July 1, 2029 and July 1,
2030 for the purpose of providing funding for the following organization in the following amounts:
Agency Fiscal Year 27/28 Fiscal Year 28/29 Fiscal Year 29/30 Fiscal Year 30/31
Historical Preservation Alliance $100,000 $103,000 $106,090 $109,272
Totals $100,000 $103,000 $106,090 $109,272
The additional levy allowing for 5% delinquencies and 2% discount for fiscal year beginning July 1, 2027, shall be on Class I property 0.06 cents; on
Class II property 0.12 cents; and on Class III and Class IV property 0.24 cents; per $100.00 valuation.
The additional levy allowing for 5% delinquencies and 2% discount for fiscal year beginning July 1, 2028, shall be on Class I property 0.06 cents; on
Class II property 0.12 cents; and on Class III and Class IV property 0.24 cents; per $100.00 valuation.
The additional levy allowing for 5% delinquencies and 2% discount for fiscal year beginning July 1, 2029, shall be on Class I property 0.06 cents; on
Class II property 0.12 cents; and on Class III and Class IV property 0.24 cents; per $100.00 valuation.
The additional levy allowing for 5% delinquencies and 2% discount for fiscal year beginning July 1, 2030, shall be on Class I property 0.07 cents; on
Class II property 0.14 cents; and on Class III and Class IV property 0.28 cents; per $100.00 valuation.
That in the event the separate and aggregate assessed valuation of each class of taxable property within the County of Harrison increases during the
term of the special levy, the levy rate shall be adjusted so that the projected tax collection will not exceed the maximum sum of: $100,000 for the
fiscal year beginning July 1, 2027; $103,000 for the fiscal year beginning July 1, 2028; $106,090 for the fiscal year beginning July 1, 2029; and
$109,272 for the fiscal year beginning July 1, 2030.
Examples of authorized uses of levy funds are as follows: General operating expenses, building, infrastructure, and equipment maintenance and
repairs, promotion of tourism and historical preservation, acquisition and upgrades of equipment, property or structures, improvements and
modifications to land and facilities, and matching funds to obtain federal funds or other available funds. The Harrison County Commission will each
year provide the amount of funds from the levy to the Historical Preservation Alliance in the amounts as set forth in this order. Further, in relation to
the expenditure of funds, the Historical Preservation Alliance shall quarterly account for all expenditures, costs, and requisitions to the Harrison
County Commission; and no levy funds shall be distributed to the Historical Preservation Alliance without written proof being provided to, and
reviewed by, the Harrison County Commission. The Historical Preservation Alliance shall reimburse the Harrison County Commission for any and all
levy funds determined to have been expended for an unauthorized purpose.
In the event the Historical Preservation Alliance would cease to exist, for whatever reason, or, if the amount of funds collected exceed the
approximate amount, the Harrison County Commission will follow all necessary legal and governmental requirements to ensure that any funding
previously allocated to said entity may be repurposed; and that such finding may be repurposed for use in county special and/or improvements
projects; or otherwise that such funds be eliminated from collection consistent with West Virginia law.
For, the additional levy to provide funding for The Historical Preservation Alliance
Against, the additional levy to provide funding for The Historical Preservation Alliance

only citizens of the United States who are

OFFICIAL LEVY BALLOT
Special Levy Election
Historical Preservation Alliance
According to the Order of the Harrison County Commission, adopted on the 31st day of August, 2026, A Special election will be held to authorize
additional levies, allowing for 5% delinquencies and 2% discount, for the fiscal years beginning July 1, 2027, July 1, 2028, July 1, 2029 and July 1,
2030 for the purpose of providing funding for the following organization in the following amounts:
Agency Fiscal Year 27/28 Fiscal Year 28/29 Fiscal Year 29/30 Fiscal Year 30/31
Historical Preservation Alliance $100,000 $103,000 $106,090 $109,272
Totals $100,000 $103,000 $106,090 $109,272
The additional levy allowing for 5% delinquencies and 2% discount for fiscal year beginning July 1, 2027, shall be on Class I property 0.06 cents; on
Class II property 0.12 cents; and on Class III and Class IV property 0.24 cents; per $100.00 valuation.
The additional levy allowing for 5% delinquencies and 2% discount for fiscal year beginning July 1, 2028, shall be on Class I property 0.06 cents; on
Class II property 0.12 cents; and on Class III and Class IV property 0.24 cents; per $100.00 valuation.
The additional levy allowing for 5% delinquencies and 2% discount for fiscal year beginning July 1, 2029, shall be on Class I property 0.06 cents; on
Class II property 0.12 cents; and on Class III and Class IV property 0.24 cents; per $100.00 valuation.
The additional levy allowing for 5% delinquencies and 2% discount for fiscal year beginning July 1, 2030, shall be on Class I property 0.07 cents; on
Class II property 0.14 cents; and on Class III and Class IV property 0.28 cents; per $100.00 valuation.
That in the event the separate and aggregate assessed valuation of each class of taxable property within the County of Harrison increases during the
term of the special levy, the levy rate shall be adjusted so that the projected tax collection will not exceed the maximum sum of: $100,000 for the
fiscal year beginning July 1, 2027; $103,000 for the fiscal year beginning July 1, 2028; $106,090 for the fiscal year beginning July 1, 2029; and
$109,272 for the fiscal year beginning July 1, 2030.
Examples of authorized uses of levy funds are as follows: General operating expenses, building, infrastructure, and equipment maintenance and
repairs, promotion of tourism and historical preservation, acquisition and upgrades of equipment, property or structures, improvements and
modifications to land and facilities, and matching funds to obtain federal funds or other available funds. The Harrison County Commission will each
year provide the amount of funds from the levy to the Historical Preservation Alliance in the amounts as set forth in this order. Further, in relation to
the expenditure of funds, the Historical Preservation Alliance shall quarterly account for all expenditures, costs, and requisitions to the Harrison
County Commission; and no levy funds shall be distributed to the Historical Preservation Alliance without written proof being provided to, and
reviewed by, the Harrison County Commission. The Historical Preservation Alliance shall reimburse the Harrison County Commission for any and all
levy funds determined to have been expended for an unauthorized purpose.
In the event the Historical Preservation Alliance would cease to exist, for whatever reason, or, if the amount of funds collected exceed the
approximate amount, the Harrison County Commission will follow all necessary legal and governmental requirements to ensure that any funding
previously allocated to said entity may be repurposed; and that such finding may be repurposed for use in county special and/or improvements
projects; or otherwise that such funds be eliminated from collection consistent with West Virginia law.
For, the additional levy to provide funding for The Historical Preservation Alliance
Against, the additional levy to provide funding for The Historical Preservation Alliance
Special Levy Election
Community Health Organizations within Harrison County
Lowe Public Library, Southern Area Public Library, Nutter Fort Public Library
Harrison County Commission for Harrison County Special Projects
According to the Order of the Harrison County Commission, adopted on the 31st day of August, 2026, A Special election will be held to authorize
additional levies, allowing for 5% delinquencies and 2% discount, for the fiscal years beginning July 1, 2027, July 1, 2028, July 1, 2029 and July 1,
2030 for the purpose of providing for the continued operation of the following organizations in the following amounts:
Agency Fiscal Year 27/28 Fiscal Year 28/29 Fiscal Year 29/30 Fiscal Year 30/31
Community Health $880,000 $906,400 $933,592 $961,600
Lowe Public Library $10,000 $10,300 $10,609 $10,927
Southern Area Public Library $10,000 $10,300 $10,609 $10,927
Nutter Fort Public Library $10,000 $10,300 $10,609 $10,927
Harrison County Special Projects $450,000 $463,500 $477,405 $491,727
Totals $1,360,000 $1,400,800 $1,442,824 $1,486,108
The additional levy allowing for 5% delinquencies and 2% discount for fiscal year beginning July 1, 2027, shall be on Class I property 0.88 cents; on
Class II property 1.76 cents; and on Class III and Class IV property 3.52 cents; per $100.00 valuation.
The additional levy allowing for 5% delinquencies and 2% discount for fiscal year beginning July 1, 2028, shall be on Class I property 0.91 cents; on
Class II property 1.82 cents; and on Class III and Class IV property 3.64 cents; per $100.00 valuation.
The additional levy allowing for 5% delinquencies and 2% discount for fiscal year beginning July 1, 2029, shall be on Class I property 0.94 cents; on
Class II property 1.88 cents; and on Class III and Class IV property 3.76 cents; per $100.00 valuation.
The additional levy allowing for 5% delinquencies and 2% discount for fiscal year beginning July 1, 2030, shall be on Class I property 0.96 cents; on
Class II property 1.92 cents; and on Class III and Class IV property 3.84 cents; per $100.00 valuation.
That in the event the separate and aggregate assessed valuation of each class of taxable property within the County of Harrison increases during the
term of the special levy, the levy rate shall be adjusted so that the projected tax collection will not exceed the maximum sum of: $1,360,000 for the
fiscal year beginning July 1, 2027; $1,400,800 for the fiscal year beginning July 1, 2028; $1,442,824 for the fiscal year beginning July 1, 2029; and
$1,486,108 for the fiscal year beginning July 1, 2030.
Examples of acceptable usage of funds are as follows: General operating expenses, building, infrastructure, and equipment maintenance and
repairs, acquisition and upgrades of equipment, property or structures, improvements and modifications to land and facilities, and matching funds to
obtain federal funds or other available funds. The Harrison County Commission will each year provide the amount of funds from the levy to the
Clarksburg/Harrison Health Department, Lowe Public Library, Southern Area Public Library, Nutter Fort Public Library, and Harrison County for
Harrison County Special Projects. Funds for the remaining organizations will be distributed by the Clarksburg/Harrison Health Department to each
organization in the amounts as set forth in this order.
In the event any of these entities would cease to exist, for whatever reason, or, if the amount of funds collected exceed the approximate amount, the
Harrison County Commission will follow all necessary legal and governmental requirements to ensure that any funding previously allocated to said
entity may be redistributed to one or more of the remaining entities, on an equitable basis. If the amount of funds collected is less than the
anticipated approximate amount, each beneficiarys distribution amount will be reduced equitably.
In the event that sufficient State, Federal, or other special funds become available to provide monies for any of the above purposes, or the Harrison
County Commissions anticipated expenses for each stated purpose are lower than projected, the levy monies specified for these purposes may be
used for the general operation and improvement of the county. The Harrison County Commission is hereby authorized and empowered to expend,
during the term of this Levy, the funds, if any, occurring in excess of the amount needed for any of the above stated purposes for the enrichment,
supplementation, improvement and advancement of all county programs, operations and facilities in Harrison County, and other purposes pertinent
to the operation of the county.
Yes, for continued funding for Harrison County Commission for Community Health Organizations within Harrison County, Lowe
Public Library, Southern Area Public Library, Nutter Fort Public Library, and the Harrison County Commission for Harrison County
Special Projects
No, for continued funding for Harrison County Commission for Community Health Organizations within Harrison County, Lowe
Public Library, Southern Area Public Library, Nutter Fort Public Library, and the Harrison County Commission for Harrison County
Special Projects

Harrison County Special Projects $450,000 $463,500 $477,405 $491,727
Totals $1,360,000 $1,400,800 $1,442,824 $1,486,108
The additional levy allowing for 5% delinquencies and 2% discount for fiscal year beginning July 1, 2027, shall be on Class I property 0.88 cents; on
Class II property 1.76 cents; and on Class III and Class IV property 3.52 cents; per $100.00 valuation.
The additional levy allowing for 5% delinquencies and 2% discount for fiscal year beginning July 1, 2028, shall be on Class I property 0.91 cents; on
Class II property 1.82 cents; and on Class III and Class IV property 3.64 cents; per $100.00 valuation.
The additional levy allowing for 5% delinquencies and 2% discount for fiscal year beginning July 1, 2029, shall be on Class I property 0.94 cents; on
Class II property 1.88 cents; and on Class III and Class IV property 3.76 cents; per $100.00 valuation.
The additional levy allowing for 5% delinquencies and 2% discount for fiscal year beginning July 1, 2030, shall be on Class I property 0.96 cents; on
Class II property 1.92 cents; and on Class III and Class IV property 3.84 cents; per $100.00 valuation.
That in the event the separate and aggregate assessed valuation of each class of taxable property within the County of Harrison increases during the
term of the special levy, the levy rate shall be adjusted so that the projected tax collection will not exceed the maximum sum of: $1,360,000 for the
fiscal year beginning July 1, 2027; $1,400,800 for the fiscal year beginning July 1, 2028; $1,442,824 for the fiscal year beginning July 1, 2029; and
$1,486,108 for the fiscal year beginning July 1, 2030.
Examples of acceptable usage of funds are as follows: General operating expenses, building, infrastructure, and equipment maintenance and
repairs, acquisition and upgrades of equipment, property or structures, improvements and modifications to land and facilities, and matching funds to
obtain federal funds or other available funds. The Harrison County Commission will each year provide the amount of funds from the levy to the
Clarksburg/Harrison Health Department, Lowe Public Library, Southern Area Public Library, Nutter Fort Public Library, and Harrison County for
Harrison County Special Projects. Funds for the remaining organizations will be distributed by the Clarksburg/Harrison Health Department to each
organization in the amounts as set forth in this order.
In the event any of these entities would cease to exist, for whatever reason, or, if the amount of funds collected exceed the approximate amount, the
Harrison County Commission will follow all necessary legal and governmental requirements to ensure that any funding previously allocated to said
entity may be redistributed to one or more of the remaining entities, on an equitable basis. If the amount of funds collected is less than the
anticipated approximate amount, each beneficiarys distribution amount will be reduced equitably.
In the event that sufficient State, Federal, or other special funds become available to provide monies for any of the above purposes, or the Harrison
County Commissions anticipated expenses for each stated purpose are lower than projected, the levy monies specified for these purposes may be
used for the general operation and improvement of the county. The Harrison County Commission is hereby authorized and empowered to expend,
during the term of this Levy, the funds, if any, occurring in excess of the amount needed for any of the above stated purposes for the enrichment,
supplementation, improvement and advancement of all county programs, operations and facilities in Harrison County, and other purposes pertinent
to the operation of the county.
Yes, for continued funding for Harrison County Commission for Community Health Organizations within Harrison County, Lowe
Public Library, Southern Area Public Library, Nutter Fort Public Library, and the Harrison County Commission for Harrison County
Special Projects
No, for continued funding for Harrison County Commission for Community Health Organizations within Harrison County, Lowe
Public Library, Southern Area Public Library, Nutter Fort Public Library, and the Harrison County Commission for Harrison County
Special Projects

Amendment
Summary of Purpose: This amendment
provides that in all elections held in West Virginia
only citizens of the United States who are
citizens of this state are qualified to vote.

OFFICIAL LEVY BALLOT
Special Levy Election
Historical Preservation Alliance
According to the Order of the Harrison County Commission, adopted on the 31st day of August, 2026, A Special election will be held to authorize
additional levies, allowing for 5% delinquencies and 2% discount, for the fiscal years beginning July 1, 2027, July 1, 2028, July 1, 2029 and July 1,
2030 for the purpose of providing funding for the following organization in the following amounts:
Agency Fiscal Year 27/28 Fiscal Year 28/29 Fiscal Year 29/30 Fiscal Year 30/31
Historical Preservation Alliance $100,000 $103,000 $106,090 $109,272
Totals $100,000 $103,000 $106,090 $109,272
The additional levy allowing for 5% delinquencies and 2% discount for fiscal year beginning July 1, 2027, shall be on Class I property 0.06 cents; on
Class II property 0.12 cents; and on Class III and Class IV property 0.24 cents; per $100.00 valuation.
The additional levy allowing for 5% delinquencies and 2% discount for fiscal year beginning July 1, 2028, shall be on Class I property 0.06 cents; on
Class II property 0.12 cents; and on Class III and Class IV property 0.24 cents; per $100.00 valuation.
The additional levy allowing for 5% delinquencies and 2% discount for fiscal year beginning July 1, 2029, shall be on Class I property 0.06 cents; on
Class II property 0.12 cents; and on Class III and Class IV property 0.24 cents; per $100.00 valuation.
The additional levy allowing for 5% delinquencies and 2% discount for fiscal year beginning July 1, 2030, shall be on Class I property 0.07 cents; on
Class II property 0.14 cents; and on Class III and Class IV property 0.28 cents; per $100.00 valuation.
That in the event the separate and aggregate assessed valuation of each class of taxable property within the County of Harrison increases during the
term of the special levy, the levy rate shall be adjusted so that the projected tax collection will not exceed the maximum sum of: $100,000 for the
fiscal year beginning July 1, 2027; $103,000 for the fiscal year beginning July 1, 2028; $106,090 for the fiscal year beginning July 1, 2029; and
$109,272 for the fiscal year beginning July 1, 2030.
Examples of authorized uses of levy funds are as follows: General operating expenses, building, infrastructure, and equipment maintenance and
repairs, promotion of tourism and historical preservation, acquisition and upgrades of equipment, property or structures, improvements and
modifications to land and facilities, and matching funds to obtain federal funds or other available funds. The Harrison County Commission will each
year provide the amount of funds from the levy to the Historical Preservation Alliance in the amounts as set forth in this order. Further, in relation to
the expenditure of funds, the Historical Preservation Alliance shall quarterly account for all expenditures, costs, and requisitions to the Harrison
County Commission; and no levy funds shall be distributed to the Historical Preservation Alliance without written proof being provided to, and
reviewed by, the Harrison County Commission. The Historical Preservation Alliance shall reimburse the Harrison County Commission for any and all
levy funds determined to have been expended for an unauthorized purpose.
In the event the Historical Preservation Alliance would cease to exist, for whatever reason, or, if the amount of funds collected exceed the
approximate amount, the Harrison County Commission will follow all necessary legal and governmental requirements to ensure that any funding
previously allocated to said entity may be repurposed; and that such finding may be repurposed for use in county special and/or improvements
projects; or otherwise that such funds be eliminated from collection consistent with West Virginia law.
For, the additional levy to provide funding for The Historical Preservation Alliance
Against, the additional levy to provide funding for The Historical Preservation Alliance

OFFICIAL LEVY BALLOT
Special Levy Election
Historical Preservation Alliance
According to the Order of the Harrison County Commission, adopted on the 31st day of August, 2026, A Special election will be held to authorize
additional levies, allowing for 5% delinquencies and 2% discount, for the fiscal years beginning July 1, 2027, July 1, 2028, July 1, 2029 and July 1,
2030 for the purpose of providing funding for the following organization in the following amounts:
Agency Fiscal Year 27/28 Fiscal Year 28/29 Fiscal Year 29/30 Fiscal Year 30/31
Historical Preservation Alliance $100,000 $103,000 $106,090 $109,272
Totals $100,000 $103,000 $106,090 $109,272
The additional levy allowing for 5% delinquencies and 2% discount for fiscal year beginning July 1, 2027, shall be on Class I property 0.06 cents; on
Class II property 0.12 cents; and on Class III and Class IV property 0.24 cents; per $100.00 valuation.
The additional levy allowing for 5% delinquencies and 2% discount for fiscal year beginning July 1, 2028, shall be on Class I property 0.06 cents; on
Class II property 0.12 cents; and on Class III and Class IV property 0.24 cents; per $100.00 valuation.
The additional levy allowing for 5% delinquencies and 2% discount for fiscal year beginning July 1, 2029, shall be on Class I property 0.06 cents; on
Class II property 0.12 cents; and on Class III and Class IV property 0.24 cents; per $100.00 valuation.
The additional levy allowing for 5% delinquencies and 2% discount for fiscal year beginning July 1, 2030, shall be on Class I property 0.07 cents; on
Class II property 0.14 cents; and on Class III and Class IV property 0.28 cents; per $100.00 valuation.
That in the event the separate and aggregate assessed valuation of each class of taxable property within the County of Harrison increases during the
term of the special levy, the levy rate shall be adjusted so that the projected tax collection will not exceed the maximum sum of: $100,000 for the
fiscal year beginning July 1, 2027; $103,000 for the fiscal year beginning July 1, 2028; $106,090 for the fiscal year beginning July 1, 2029; and
$109,272 for the fiscal year beginning July 1, 2030.
Examples of authorized uses of levy funds are as follows: General operating expenses, building, infrastructure, and equipment maintenance and
repairs, promotion of tourism and historical preservation, acquisition and upgrades of equipment, property or structures, improvements and
modifications to land and facilities, and matching funds to obtain federal funds or other available funds. The Harrison County Commission will each
year provide the amount of funds from the levy to the Historical Preservation Alliance in the amounts as set forth in this order. Further, in relation to
the expenditure of funds, the Historical Preservation Alliance shall quarterly account for all expenditures, costs, and requisitions to the Harrison
County Commission; and no levy funds shall be distributed to the Historical Preservation Alliance without written proof being provided to, and
reviewed by, the Harrison County Commission. The Historical Preservation Alliance shall reimburse the Harrison County Commission for any and all
levy funds determined to have been expended for an unauthorized purpose.
In the event the Historical Preservation Alliance would cease to exist, for whatever reason, or, if the amount of funds collected exceed the
approximate amount, the Harrison County Commission will follow all necessary legal and governmental requirements to ensure that any funding
previously allocated to said entity may be repurposed; and that such finding may be repurposed for use in county special and/or improvements
projects; or otherwise that such funds be eliminated from collection consistent with West Virginia law.
For, the additional levy to provide funding for The Historical Preservation Alliance
Against, the additional levy to provide funding for The Historical Preservation Alliance
Special Levy Election
Community Health Organizations within Harrison County
Lowe Public Library, Southern Area Public Library, Nutter Fort Public Library
Harrison County Commission for Harrison County Special Projects
According to the Order of the Harrison County Commission, adopted on the 31st day of August, 2026, A Special election will be held to authorize
additional levies, allowing for 5% delinquencies and 2% discount, for the fiscal years beginning July 1, 2027, July 1, 2028, July 1, 2029 and July 1,
2030 for the purpose of providing for the continued operation of the following organizations in the following amounts:
Agency Fiscal Year 27/28 Fiscal Year 28/29 Fiscal Year 29/30 Fiscal Year 30/31
Community Health $880,000 $906,400 $933,592 $961,600
Lowe Public Library $10,000 $10,300 $10,609 $10,927
Southern Area Public Library $10,000 $10,300 $10,609 $10,927
Nutter Fort Public Library $10,000 $10,300 $10,609 $10,927
Harrison County Special Projects $450,000 $463,500 $477,405 $491,727
Totals $1,360,000 $1,400,800 $1,442,824 $1,486,108
The additional levy allowing for 5% delinquencies and 2% discount for fiscal year beginning July 1, 2027, shall be on Class I property 0.88 cents; on
Class II property 1.76 cents; and on Class III and Class IV property 3.52 cents; per $100.00 valuation.
The additional levy allowing for 5% delinquencies and 2% discount for fiscal year beginning July 1, 2028, shall be on Class I property 0.91 cents; on
Class II property 1.82 cents; and on Class III and Class IV property 3.64 cents; per $100.00 valuation.
The additional levy allowing for 5% delinquencies and 2% discount for fiscal year beginning July 1, 2029, shall be on Class I property 0.94 cents; on
Class II property 1.88 cents; and on Class III and Class IV property 3.76 cents; per $100.00 valuation.
The additional levy allowing for 5% delinquencies and 2% discount for fiscal year beginning July 1, 2030, shall be on Class I property 0.96 cents; on
Class II property 1.92 cents; and on Class III and Class IV property 3.84 cents; per $100.00 valuation.
That in the event the separate and aggregate assessed valuation of each class of taxable property within the County of Harrison increases during the
term of the special levy, the levy rate shall be adjusted so that the projected tax collection will not exceed the maximum sum of: $1,360,000 for the
fiscal year beginning July 1, 2027; $1,400,800 for the fiscal year beginning July 1, 2028; $1,442,824 for the fiscal year beginning July 1, 2029; and
$1,486,108 for the fiscal year beginning July 1, 2030.
Examples of acceptable usage of funds are as follows: General operating expenses, building, infrastructure, and equipment maintenance and
repairs, acquisition and upgrades of equipment, property or structures, improvements and modifications to land and facilities, and matching funds to
obtain federal funds or other available funds. The Harrison County Commission will each year provide the amount of funds from the levy to the
Clarksburg/Harrison Health Department, Lowe Public Library, Southern Area Public Library, Nutter Fort Public Library, and Harrison County for
Harrison County Special Projects. Funds for the remaining organizations will be distributed by the Clarksburg/Harrison Health Department to each
organization in the amounts as set forth in this order.
In the event any of these entities would cease to exist, for whatever reason, or, if the amount of funds collected exceed the approximate amount, the
Harrison County Commission will follow all necessary legal and governmental requirements to ensure that any funding previously allocated to said
entity may be redistributed to one or more of the remaining entities, on an equitable basis. If the amount of funds collected is less than the
anticipated approximate amount, each beneficiarys distribution amount will be reduced equitably.
In the event that sufficient State, Federal, or other special funds become available to provide monies for any of the above purposes, or the Harrison
County Commissions anticipated expenses for each stated purpose are lower than projected, the levy monies specified for these purposes may be
used for the general operation and improvement of the county. The Harrison County Commission is hereby authorized and empowered to expend,
during the term of this Levy, the funds, if any, occurring in excess of the amount needed for any of the above stated purposes for the enrichment,
supplementation, improvement and advancement of all county programs, operations and facilities in Harrison County, and other purposes pertinent
to the operation of the county.
Yes, for continued funding for Harrison County Commission for Community Health Organizations within Harrison County, Lowe
Public Library, Southern Area Public Library, Nutter Fort Public Library, and the Harrison County Commission for Harrison County
Special Projects
No, for continued funding for Harrison County Commission for Community Health Organizations within Harrison County, Lowe
Public Library, Southern Area Public Library, Nutter Fort Public Library, and the Harrison County Commission for Harrison County
Special Projects

term of the special levy, the levy rate shall be adjusted so that the projected tax collection will not exceed the maximum sum of: $1,360,000 for the
fiscal year beginning July 1, 2027; $1,400,800 for the fiscal year beginning July 1, 2028; $1,442,824 for the fiscal year beginning July 1, 2029; and
$1,486,108 for the fiscal year beginning July 1, 2030.
Examples of acceptable usage of funds are as follows: General operating expenses, building, infrastructure, and equipment maintenance and
repairs, acquisition and upgrades of equipment, property or structures, improvements and modifications to land and facilities, and matching funds to
obtain federal funds or other available funds. The Harrison County Commission will each year provide the amount of funds from the levy to the
Clarksburg/Harrison Health Department, Lowe Public Library, Southern Area Public Library, Nutter Fort Public Library, and Harrison County for
Harrison County Special Projects. Funds for the remaining organizations will be distributed by the Clarksburg/Harrison Health Department to each
organization in the amounts as set forth in this order.
In the event any of these entities would cease to exist, for whatever reason, or, if the amount of funds collected exceed the approximate amount, the
Harrison County Commission will follow all necessary legal and governmental requirements to ensure that any funding previously allocated to said
entity may be redistributed to one or more of the remaining entities, on an equitable basis. If the amount of funds collected is less than the
anticipated approximate amount, each beneficiarys distribution amount will be reduced equitably.
In the event that sufficient State, Federal, or other special funds become available to provide monies for any of the above purposes, or the Harrison
County Commissions anticipated expenses for each stated purpose are lower than projected, the levy monies specified for these purposes may be
used for the general operation and improvement of the county. The Harrison County Commission is hereby authorized and empowered to expend,
during the term of this Levy, the funds, if any, occurring in excess of the amount needed for any of the above stated purposes for the enrichment,
supplementation, improvement and advancement of all county programs, operations and facilities in Harrison County, and other purposes pertinent
to the operation of the county.
Yes, for continued funding for Harrison County Commission for Community Health Organizations within Harrison County, Lowe
Public Library, Southern Area Public Library, Nutter Fort Public Library, and the Harrison County Commission for Harrison County
Special Projects
No, for continued funding for Harrison County Commission for Community Health Organizations within Harrison County, Lowe
Public Library, Southern Area Public Library, Nutter Fort Public Library, and the Harrison County Commission for Harrison County
Special Projects

AMENDMENTS
Amendment No. 1: Citizenship Requirement
to Vote in West Virginia Elections
Amendment
Summary of Purpose: This amendment
provides that in all elections held in West Virginia
only citizens of the United States who are
citizens of this state are qualified to vote.
Instructions to Voters:
To vote in favor of the Amendment submitted,
darken the oval next to "FOR". To vote against
the Amendment, darken the oval next to
"AGAINST".
